# Claudeball announcer: recording script

_Generated by `npm run announcer:script` from the game's own vocabulary. Read the **bold** text out loud; the grey text is how the game writes it. Do not read the direction; do what it says._

| set | lines | est. audio | sessions |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| PILOT | 134 | 10.5 min | P1, P2 |
| CORE | 587 | 57.1 min | C1, C2, C3, C4, C5 |
| EXTENDED | 1376 | 70.9 min | E1, E2, E3, E4, E5, E6 |

Styles: **calm** easy descriptive, **building** rising tension, **excited**, **peak** the big call, **deadpan** dry colour commentary, **crisp** PA / umpire, **deflated** the groan.
